### [1] [The structure of networks that evolve under a combination of growth, via node addition and random attachment, and contraction, via random node deletion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.10027)
*Barak Budnick,Ofer Biham,Eytan Katzav*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文对同时包含节点添加（随机连接）和节点删除（随机删除）的网络演化模型进行了解析分析，通过主方程得到了时变度分布P_t(k)的闭式解。该分布包含随时间衰减的初始项和渐近分布P_st(k)。在纯增长情形下P_st(k)呈指数分布；在混合情形下呈泊松尾部。收缩情形下存在两个不同收敛区域，临界点位于η=-1/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的许多网络不仅会通过添加节点和边而增长，也会因节点移除而收缩（如社交网络用户流失、生物网络细胞凋亡等）。然而大多数网络演化研究聚焦于纯增长过程，对增长与收缩并存的网络拓扑结构缺乏解析层面的深入理解。

Method: 构建一个在每个时间步以概率P_add执行节点添加与随机连接、以概率P_del执行随机节点删除的网络演化模型，用参数η=P_add-P_del刻画增长与收缩的平衡关系。利用主方程（master equation）方法推导出时变度分布P_t(k)的闭式表达式。

Result: 得到P_t(k)闭式解，包含初始分布P_0(k)的衰减项和渐近分布P_st(k)。当η=1（纯增长）时P_st(k)为指数分布；当-1<η<1时P_st(k)为泊松项之和并呈泊松型尾部。在0<η<1时P_t(k)收敛到P_st(k)。在-1<η<0时存在两个区域：-1/3<η<0时快速收敛，-1<η<-1/3时收敛极慢，仅在网络消亡前不久才接近P_st(k)。

Conclusion: 该工作首次对增长-收缩并存的网络模型给出了度分布的完整解析解，揭示了收缩率超越临界值（η<-1/3）时网络会经历一个漫长的"准稳态"阶段，之后才迅速趋于渐近分布并消亡，这一临界现象对理解现实动态网络的演化具有重要意义。

Abstract: We present analytical results for the emerging structure of networks that evolve via a combination of growth (by node addition and random attachment) and contraction (by random node deletion). To this end we consider a network model in which at each time step a node addition and random attachment step takes place with probability $P_{add}$ and a random node deletion step takes place with probability $P_{del}=1-P_{add}$. The balance between the growth and contraction processes is captured by the parameter $η=P_{add}-P_{del}$. The case of pure network growth is described by $η=1$. In case that $0<η<1$ the rate of node addition exceeds the rate of node deletion and the overall process is of network growth. In the opposite case, where $-1<η<0$, the overall process is of network contraction, while in the special case of $η=0$ the expected size of the network remains fixed, apart from fluctuations. Using the master equation we obtain a closed form expression for the time dependent degree distribution $P_t(k)$. The degree distribution $P_t(k)$ includes a term that depends on the initial degree distribution $P_0(k)$, which decays as time evolves, and an asymptotic distribution $P_{st}(k)$. In the case of pure network growth ($η=1$) the asymptotic distribution $P_{st}(k)$ follows an exponential distribution, while for $-1<η<1$ it consists of a sum of Poisson-like terms and exhibits a Poisson-like tail. In the case of overall network growth ($0 < η< 1$) the degree distribution $P_t(k)$ eventually converges to $P_{st}(k)$. In the case of overall network contraction ($-1 < η< 0$) we identify two different regimes. For $-1/3 < η< 0$ the degree distribution $P_t(k)$ quickly converges towards $P_{st}(k)$. In contrast, for $-1 < η< -1/3$ the convergence of $P_t(k)$ is initially very slow and it gets closer to $P_{st}(k)$ only shortly before the network vanishes.

</details>


### [2] [Exact expressions of correlation functions between two spins in the boundary row of the two-dimensional rectangular Ising model with periodic-free boundary conditions and finite size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19274)
*Mei Tao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文推导了二维矩形Ising模型中边界行上两个自旋之间关联函数的精确表达式，该模型采用周期-自由边界条件且为有限尺寸。作者证明了在热力学极限下，所得到的表达式与先前研究者给出的形式一致，并通过图示说明了不同尺寸对关联函数的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维Ising模型是统计物理中最经典的模型之一，其关联函数是理解相变和临界现象的核心量。虽然先前研究者在热力学极限下已经给出了关联函数的表达式，但在有限尺寸下的精确表达式仍是一个值得解决的问题，特别是对于具有混合边界条件（周期-自由）的矩形系统。

Method: 作者采用解析方法推导了具有周期-自由边界条件的二维矩形Ising模型边界行上两自旋间关联函数的精确（封闭）表达式。他们分析了这些精确表达式的性质，包括热力学极限下取极限顺序（L和N两个参数）对结果的影响，并验证了与先前文献中热力学极限表达式的等价性。

Result: 获得了有限尺寸下边界自旋关联函数的精确表达式；展示了Ising模型随温度降低涌现长程序的基本性质；证明热力学极限下关联函数的表达式依赖于L和N两个参数取极限的先后顺序；通过图示说明了不同系统尺寸对关联函数的影响；证明了所得表达式在热力学极限下与先前研究者给出的表达式形式一致。

Conclusion: 本文提供了有限尺寸二维矩形Ising模型（周期-自由边界条件）边界自旋关联函数的精确表达式，不仅验证了与热力学极限下已知结果的一致性，还揭示了极限顺序依赖等有限尺寸效应，对理解Ising模型的长程序涌现和边界效应具有理论价值。

Abstract: We present exact expressions of correlation functions between two spins in the boundary row of the two-dimensional rectangular Ising model with periodic-free boundary conditions and finite size. Some properties of exact expressions obtained are discussed. The fundamental property of the Ising model that the long range order emerge as the temperature decrease is shown clearly; expressions of the correlation functions in the thermodynamic limit varies depending on the order of taking limits of two parameters L and N; the impact of different sizes on correlation functions is illustrated with the aid of diagrams. Expressions of the correlation function discussed in this paper in the thermodynamic limit has been presented by previous researchers, and we prove that expressions obtained in this article is identical in form to expressions provided by previous researchers in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [3] [Quantum Rényi-Jarzynski Equality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19320)
*Benjamin Bobell,Mert Okyay,Rahul Nandkishore*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用资源理论方法推导出一种非破坏性的量子Jarzynski等式，该等式以任意浴可观测量的结果为条件，得到Rényi-Jarzynski等式，可用Rényi k散度量化有限浴在非绝热驱动下偏离平衡的程度，并作为量子最优控制中最小化浴漂移的可调代价函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子Jarzynski等式依赖于破坏相干性的测量协议，这限制了其在量子信息处理中的应用。作者希望发展一种不破坏量子相干性的Jarzynski等式框架，以更好地描述有限量子系统中浴的偏离平衡行为，并为量子最优控制提供新的代价函数。

Method: 采用资源理论（resource-theoretic）方法，推导出一个以任意浴可观测量结果为条件的非破坏性量子Jarzynski等式。通过引入Rényi k散度来量化有限浴在非绝热驱动下的偏离平衡程度，并将该等式作为量子最优控制中的可调代价函数，应用于态制备和量子门设计等场景。

Result: 推导出Rényi-Jarzynski等式，证明了它可作为量子最优控制中最小化浴漂移的可调代价函数。在玩具模型中观察到在某个临界k值下竞争极小值之间发生转变，表明Rényi阶数可以调节对浴分布不同区域的灵敏度。此外发现当驱动参数随浴能级变化时，最小化浴漂移要求产生系统-浴纠缠。

Conclusion: Rényi-Jarzynski等式为非破坏性量子热力学提供了一种新工具，通过可调节的Rényi阶数k，可以灵活控制对浴分布不同区域的灵敏度，进而为有限量子系统中的态制备和量子门设计提供优化方案，同时揭示了系统-浴纠缠在最小化浴漂移中的关键作用。

Abstract: The Jarzynski equality provides a strict link between nonequilibrium work and equilibrium free energy changes. Its typical quantum formulations, however, rely on measurement protocols that destroy coherence. In this Letter, we use the resource-theoretic approach to derive a non-destructive quantum Jarzynski equality conditioned on the outcomes of an arbitrary bath observable. This yields the Rényi-Jarzynski equality, which quantifies a finite bath's drift from equilibrium under a non-adiabatic drive via the Rényi $k$-divergence. We further demonstrate that the Rényi-Jarzynski equality provides a tunable cost function for quantum optimal control problems where minimizing bath drift is desired, such as state preparation and gate design, enabling the minimization of cross-talk in finite quantum systems. Our toy model exhibits a transition between competing minima for some critical value of $k$, illustrating how the Rényi order tunes sensitivity to different regions of a bath distribution. Strikingly, when drive parameters vary across bath energy levels, minimizing bath drift requires generating system-bath entanglement.

</details>


### [4] [State convertibility and fluctuation theorems from a dynamical reference: majorization meets martingales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19391)
*Davide Cugini,Giacomo Guarnieri*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的g(t)-majorization框架，将传统的majorization和thermo-majorization推广到任意时变参考分布，并通过对偶视角将态可转换性问题转化为一维凸序问题，证明转换允许当且仅当相对总体分布的比值由鞅连接。在此基础上推导出一个精确的参考相对熵产生涨落定理，其平均违反可通过χ²散度界来认证假设参考演化与真实参考演化之间的失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 态可转换性是决定在特定资源条件下变换是否可能的基本概念。在热力学中，物理过程需保持参考态（通常为微正则或正则形式），这转化为majorization和thermo-majorization判据，需要构建和比较依赖状态的Lorenz曲线。现有框架局限于特定参考态形式，缺乏对任意时变参考分布的普适统一处理，也缺少一种诊断参考模型失配的无模型工具。

Method: 首先将majorization概念统一并推广到任意、可能时变的参考分布g(t)，引入g(t)-majorization概念；然后构建对偶图景，将态可转换性转化为一维凸序问题，证明转换允许当且仅当相对总体分布k_j(t)/g_j(t)的实值分布由鞅连接；最后基于此推导参考相对熵产生涨落定理，通过χ²散度界给出参考误差的下界估计。

Result: 实现了majorization和thermo-majorization的统一推广，建立了态可转换性与鞅/凸序之间的等价关系，推导出精确的涨落定理，并证明可通过对涨落关系的观测违反程度获得参考误差的认证下界，该诊断无需对真实参考演化进行独立表征。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一且具有模型无关性的框架，不仅能处理任意时变参考分布下的态转换问题，还提供了一种将涨落关系失效转化为参考误差可认证下界的实用诊断工具，为热力学资源理论和随机热力学提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: State convertibility represents a fundamental concept used to determine whether a transformation is possible given a specific set of resources. Within the field of Thermodynamics, where physical process are required to preserve a reference state typically in microcanonical or canonical form, this translates into the notions of majorization and thermo-majorization ---criteria that require constructing and comparing state-dependent Lorenz curves. In this work, we firstly unify and extend these notions to an arbitrary and possibly time-dependent reference distribution $g(t)$, introducing the concept of $g(t)$-majorization; we then introduce a dual picture whereby state convertibility is turned into a one-dimensional convex-order problem, which allows us to demonstrate that a transition is admissible if and only if the associated real-valued distributions of relative populations $ k_j(t)/g_j(t)$ are connected by a martingale. Building on it, we then derive an exact fluctuation theorem for a reference-relative entropy production whose average violation certifies, through a $χ^{2}$-divergence bound, the mismatch between an assumed and the true reference evolution---a model-independent diagnostic that requires no independent characterization of the latter and turns an observed breakdown of the fluctuation relation into a certified lower bound on the reference error.

</details>


### [5] [Exact partition function of arithmetic Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19605)
*Anu Dhochak,Ken Kikuchi,Shrinit Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文给出了d维算术Ising模型(AIM)精确配分函数的紧凑公式。对于2×2系统，使用q-Hurwitz-Lerch zeta函数解析地表达了配分函数，并推导出自由能和熵的显式形式。发现熵在高温下增加，支持熵序的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算术Ising模型(AIM)的精确配分函数在之前尚缺乏简洁的解析表达式，作者旨在填补这一空白，并为理解该模型在高温区域的熵行为提供理论依据。

Method: 针对d维算术Ising模型，推导了精确配分函数的紧凑公式。对于2×2系统，利用q-Hurwitz-Lerch zeta函数进行解析表达，并由此推导出自由能和熵的显式形式。

Result: 成功获得2×2体系配分函数的解析表达式；发现熵在高温区随温度增加而增大，这一行为支持了熵序的存在。

Conclusion: 本文通过精确解析求解，揭示了算术Ising模型中熵在高温区间增大的特性，为熵序现象提供了直接的理论证据。

Abstract: We present a compact formula for the exact partition function of the $d$-dimensional arithmetic Ising model (AIM). For a $2\times2$ system, we express it analytically using the $q$-Hurwitz-Lerch zeta function and derive explicit forms for the free energy and entropy. Additionally, we find that the entropy increases at high temperatures, supporting the presence of entropic order.

</details>


### [6] [Statistical complexity from fluctuations in the information content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19485)
*Renio S. Mendes,Sergio Picoli,Evaldo M. F. Curado*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出将信息内容（C）的方差作为一种统计复杂度度量，证明其满足统计复杂度的公认标准，并与热力学和相变建立直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的统计复杂度度量往往缺乏明确的物理意义或与热力学量的直接联系，作者旨在寻找一种既能满足复杂度度量标准、又能与物理过程（如相变）建立明确对应关系的度量。

Method: 将信息内容的方差C定义为统计复杂度度量，通过理论分析验证其满足有序态与等概率态为零、中间态最大的标准，并与Boltzmann-Gibbs统计下的能量涨落和热容建立解析联系；通过二维Ising模型、混沌映射和分数高斯噪声等系统进行数值验证。

Result: C满足统计复杂度度量的公认标准；在玻尔兹曼-吉布斯统计下具有广延性并与能量波动及热容成正比；在连续相变处达到最大值（以二维Ising模型为例）；在混沌映射和分数高斯噪声中能捕捉非平凡动力学结构。

Conclusion: 信息内容的方差C是一种兼具理论严谨性和物理意义的统计复杂度度量，其与热力学量及相变的直接联系使其在复杂系统研究中具有独特优势。

Abstract: We argue that the variance of the information content ($C$), an information-theoretic quantity, can be naturally interpreted as a measure of statistical complexity. We show that $C$ satisfies widely accepted criteria for statistical complexity measures: it vanishes for both ordered and equiprobable states, while attaining maxima in intermediate regimes, typically shifted toward order. This interpretation establishes direct connections with thermodynamics and phase transitions: for systems obeying Boltzmann--Gibbs statistics, $C$ is extensive and directly proportional to energy fluctuations and heat capacity. Moreover, unlike other statistical complexity measures, it attains a maximum at continuous phase transitions, as illustrated for the two-dimensional Ising model. Applications to chaotic maps and fractional Gaussian noise further indicate that $C$ captures nontrivial dynamical structure in different classes of correlated systems.

</details>


### [7] [Splitting probabilities for Brownian motion with diffusing boundaries: Application to polymer translocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20226)
*Alexander K. Hartmann,Satya N. Majumdar,Alberto Rosso*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究聚合物链通过纳米孔的易位过程，其中链长因聚合-解聚过程而随机波动。作者将该过程映射为孔在两条扩散汇（sinks）之间进行的随机游走，解析计算了分裂概率、易位时间分布和易位完成时链长分布的精确解，发现两者均具有指数依赖于扩散常数的幂律尾部，并通过数值模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的聚合物易位研究通常假设链长为固定值，但实际生物和实验场景中链端会发生聚合和解聚反应，导致链长动态变化。理解这种动态链长对易位动力学的影响是重要的理论问题。

Method: 将聚合物易位过程映射为孔在两个可移动扩散汇之间的随机行走问题。在孔运动为扩散（扩散常数D2）时，利用精确解析方法计算分裂概率（pore先击中左或右汇的概率）、易位时间的概率分布以及易位完成时刻链长的概率分布。数值模拟用于验证解析预测。此外，对孔为分数布朗运动（Hurst指数0<H<1）的情况给出了数值结果。

Result: 分裂概率在移动汇存在时表现出比经典不可动汇情形（即链长固定）更非平凡的行为。易位时间分布和易位完成时链长分布均具有幂律尾部，其指数连续依赖于三个扩散常数D1、D2和D3。解析结果与数值模拟一致。

Conclusion: 链长波动（由聚合-解聚引起）本质性地改变了聚合物易位的统计特性，使得关键分布呈现依赖扩散参数的连续幂律指数，这与固定链长的经典结果有显著区别。对分数布朗运动的推广结果进一步拓展了该理论框架的适用范围。

Abstract: We study the translocation of a polymer chain through a nanopore where the chain length fluctuates stochastically due to the polymerization-depolymerization processes at the chain ends. We map this process to an equivalent representation where the pore performs a stochastic random-walk-like process on a line in the presence of two diffusing sinks on either side of it with diffusion constants $D_1$ and $D_3$ respectively. The translocation process terminates when the pore hits either of the two outer diffusing sinks. In the case where the pore motion itself is diffusive with diffusion constant $D_2$, we compute exactly the splitting probability that the pore hits the left (right) sink before hitting the right (left) sink. We show that the splitting probability in the presence of mobile sinks is rather nontrivial compared to the classical case of immobile sinks (the latter corresponds to the case when the chain length is fixed). Furthermore, we also compute exactly the probability distribution of the translocation time and that of the chain length at the completion time of the translocation. We show that both distributions have power law tails with exponents that depend continuously on the diffusion constants $D_1$, $D_2$ and $D_3$. We validate our analytical predictions via numerical simulations. We then present numerical results for the case when the pore performs a fractional Brownian motion with Hurst exponent $0<H<1$, while the sinks are still diffusive.

</details>


### [8] [Exact Fluctuation-Response Relations for Underdamped Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20013)
*Tan Van Vu,Van Tuan Vo,Ruicheng Bao,Keiji Saito*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者推导了一个精确的有限时间涨落-响应等式，适用于欠阻尼Langevin动力学，适用于任意时间依赖驱动和一般加性可观测量。基于该等式，他们获得响应界限和动态生成方差的变分刻画。通过选择沿不可逆概率流的微扰方向，导出一个摩擦-响应热力学不确定关系，该关系可在任意有限观测时间被饱和，从而得出总熵产生的一个精确变分原理。文中还对比了速度分辨流的传统不确定因子与摩擦-响应因子的行为差异。

Conclusion: 欠阻尼动力学中，直接连接涨落与耗散的核心量不是平均电流本身，而是“响应”。该工作建立了以响应为核心的新理论框架，统一了欠阻尼体系中的涨落、响应与耗散关系，并为总熵产生的精确估计提供了变分工具。

Abstract: Thermodynamic uncertainty relations connect current fluctuations to dissipation and are often rooted in fluctuation-response principles. In underdamped dynamics, however, conventional mean-current uncertainty relations can fail, while the underlying connection between fluctuations, response, and dissipation remains elusive. Here we uncover this structure by deriving an exact finite-time fluctuation-response equality for underdamped Langevin dynamics, valid for arbitrary time-dependent driving and general additive observables. The equality yields sharp response bounds and a variational characterization of the dynamically generated variance. Choosing the perturbation along the irreversible probability flow gives a friction-response thermodynamic uncertainty relation that can be saturated at any finite observation time, leading to an exact variational principle for the total entropy production. We further show that the conventional uncertainty factor of velocity-resolved currents can decay exponentially with dissipation even in driven free diffusion, while the friction-response factor retains its universal lower bound. These results establish response, rather than the mean current itself, as the quantity directly linking fluctuations and dissipation in underdamped dynamics.

Abstract: We report asymmetric kinetics in thermally driven metal-insulator transitions (MITs) in 1T-TaS$_2$. These results establish a general framework in which the degree of athermality controls pathway selection in first-order phase transitions.

Abstract: Motivated by a previous Ising study, we identify a ${\it higher}$ Nishimori line in the learning phase diagram of the $2D$ $q$-state Potts model $(2 < q\leq 4)$ under bond-energy measurements. This ${\it higher}$ Nishimori line meets the critical temperature line of the Potts model, in a ${\it higher}$ Nishimori critical point -- a tricritical point at finite inference strength that separates a paramagnetic, a ferromagnetic and a 'spin-glass' phase. With analytical tools, we discuss the general structure of the rich phase diagram, which contains two unstable and three stable fixed points, and obtain a number of exact results for universal quantities, including the decay exponent of the Edwards-Anderson correlator, using a Gaussian measurement protocol which allows for exact calculations. Using extensive numerical tools, we confirm these statements for a generic, discrete $q$-state measurement protocol and determine precise numerical estimates for the location of higher and ordinary Nishimori critical points as well as RG flows between the various fixed points. We also discuss the Casimir effective central charges of the critical points in the learning phase diagram, and their monotonic ${\it decrease}$ along measurement-induced RG flows, as established non-perturbatively by the c-effective theorem and its extensions, and contrast it to the monotonic increase along the corresponding RG flows in the random-bond Potts model. Finally, we discuss a general argument based on ${\it Elitzur's \; theorem}$ that establishes stability of the ordinary Nishimori critical points in their respective learning phase diagrams. Equivalently, our results describe a monitored deformed $\mathbb{Z}_q$ toric code where the tricritical ${\it higher}$ Nishimori point is an 'information' critical point that separates stable quantum, classical, and no memory phases.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文针对非马尔可夫跳跃过程（即事件率依赖于过去历史的离散事件序列），发展了一套直接的涨落-响应理论。通过将每个转移计数分解为可预测的历史依赖均值与不可预测的鞅增量，证明了这些鞅增量构成任意可观测涨落的正交完备基，并由此推导出精确的有限时间和有限频率涨落-响应关系、响应-异质性间隙以及响应-动力学不确定关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代实验中常记录非平衡动力学为离散事件序列，其事件率依赖于已实现的历史（非马尔可夫）。然而，记忆效应会破坏状态概率的闭合主方程，使得传统马尔可夫框架下的响应理论不再适用。因此迫切需要一种直接在观测事件记录上建立、不依赖闭合主方程的涨落-响应理论，以处理记忆性系统的响应预测与推断问题。

Method: 作者在事件记录层面直接分析非马尔可夫跳跃过程。核心方法包括：(1) 将每个转移计数表示为历史依赖的平均事件倾向（可预测部分）与鞅增量（不可预测随机部分）之和；(2) 证明不同转移、不同时刻的鞅增量相互正交，并构成任意观测（如电流、占据时间、事件计数）随机偏差的完整正交基；(3) 定义事件-后果核为某事件对最终可观测量的预测影响，表明其乘以事件强度即给出对相应转移率扰动的历史条件响应；(4) 通过历史平均得到响应-异质性间隙，并推导有限时间与有限频率的精确涨落-响应关系，以及响应-动力学不确定关系。

Result: 获得了强度归一化响应恰等于该事件在观测涨落中展开系数的精确识别关系，由此导出精确的有限时间与有限频率涨落-响应关系。历史平均后得到非负的响应-异质性间隙，它衡量同一事件在不同历史下后果的差异程度，并可用于检验所提出的记忆坐标是否响应充分。有限历史版本可从自发轨迹中估计，且通过限制对数率灵敏度进一步得到由动力学活性控制的响应-动力学不确定关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为记忆性（非马尔可夫）跳跃过程的响应理论建立了直接基于事件记录的数学框架。鞅增量正交分解不仅提供了清洁的涨落-响应关系，还引入了响应-异质性间隙这一新诊断工具，可用于判断记忆坐标的充分性，并最终给出受动力学活性约束的不确定关系，为实验数据的响应预测提供了可估计、可检验的理论工具。

Abstract: Modern experiments often record nonequilibrium dynamics as sequences of discrete events whose rates depend on the realized past. We develop a fluctuation-response theory for such non-Markovian jump processes directly on the observed event record. Memory can destroy a closed master equation for the state probabilities. Each transition count nevertheless obeys an exact stochastic equation. After the history-dependent mean event tendency is subtracted, the remaining random increment is a martingale increment--the part of the event that cannot be predicted from the past. Martingale increments associated with different transitions and different times are orthogonal. These increments form a complete orthogonal basis for the random deviation of any observable measured from the record, such as a current, occupation time, or event count. The coefficient of a given increment is the event-consequence kernel. It measures how that event changes the predicted final observable, relative to continuing without the event, for the particular history already realized. Multiplying this kernel by the event intensity gives the history-conditioned response to perturbing the corresponding transition rate. Thus, the intensity-normalized response is exactly the expansion coefficient of that event in the observable fluctuation. This identification yields exact finite-time and finite-frequency fluctuation-response relations. Averaging over histories leaves a nonnegative response-heterogeneity gap. The gap measures how strongly the consequence of the same event varies across histories and tests whether a proposed memory coordinate is response-sufficient. Finite-history versions can be estimated from spontaneous trajectories. Bounding the logarithmic rate sensitivity further gives response-kinetic uncertainty relations controlled by dynamical activity.

</details>

Abstract: Quadratic manifold-based model order reduction offers a viable pathway to circumvent the limitations of linear subspaces for linear control systems characterized by slow Kolmogorov $n$-width decay. However, a system-theoretic framework for constructing such quadratic approximations remains absent from the literature. This paper presents a system-agnostic, optimization-free framework for the direct construction of quadratic projection matrices. We prove that the synthesized reduced-order model matches the nonlinear moments of the full-order system and preserves its exact center manifold mapping, thereby ensuring asymptotic tracking of steady-state outputs under specific input classes. Numerical results on transport-dominated benchmark problems, namely, the one-dimensional damped wave and advection equations, show that the proposed framework achieves high-fidelity trajectory reconstruction within a significantly reduced-dimensional state space, yielding substantial online computational savings.

Abstract: In this paper, we study the structure of the value function in constrained infinite-time optimal control (CITOC) problems of piecewise affine (PWA) systems, with $\ell_1$ or $\ell_\infty$ stage cost. Existing works, such as [1], establish that the resulting value function is PWA in the state. However, existing results do not analyze whether the value function is a proper PWA function, i.e., with a finite number of affine pieces over compact sets, or whether the number of pieces can be infinite. We show that the latter case is indeed possible by means of an explicit example, which is also instrumental in establishing rigorous and easily verifiable sufficient conditions that ensure that the resulting value function is a proper PWA function. Our theoretical findings complement well-known results, e.g., the linear-quadratic case, and serve as support for recent learning-based control schemes for PWA systems. Throughout the paper, the proposed results are illustrated by means of a numerical example.

</details>

Abstract: Rather than prescribing an evaluation interval a priori, the proposed framework characterizes each evolving unsteady flow using a sequence of time windows. A consecutive-window convergence criterion is introduced to automatically identify a representative time window within its fully developed stage. The objective evaluation and discrete adjoint analysis are then carried out consistently over the identified representative time window. The framework is implemented using a regularized lattice Boltzmann method-based large-eddy simulation (LBM-LES) solver together with a partial bounce-back fluid-solid model. The consecutive-window convergence criterion is first validated using the backward-facing step flow. Cylinder-flow applications are then employed to investigate the influence of different flow regimes on the proposed framework. The wake-flow recovery problem verifies its effectiveness for unsteady topology optimization, while U-bend optimization further demonstrates its capability to identify and reorganize complex vortical structures.

Abstract: High-multiplicity flexible job shops involve many copies of a small set of job types that must be scheduled on alternative machines. Fluid relaxations provide scalable workload lower bounds, but their fractional machine allocations do not define feasible schedules for individual jobs. We show that, after a suitable finite scaling, an optimal fluid allocation can be realized exactly by a feasible repeating discrete schedule. The construction scales the fluid allocation to integer operation counts, places the resulting operations in nonoverlapping machine intervals, repeats this arrangement, and links operations across repetitions into individual jobs without moving any interval, thereby enforcing job precedence while preserving machine feasibility. For growing finite instances with the same job-type composition, even with a fixed number of extra jobs, the gap between the optimal makespan and the fluid lower bound remains bounded by a constant; hence the relative gap vanishes as the instance grows. Guided by this repeated structure, we develop type-based cyclic template replay (TCTR), which searches job-type templates using an optimization model whose size does not grow with the number of job copies and replays the selected template on the full instance. On 676 multiplicity-expanded public flexible-job-shop instances, TCTR is feasible in every case and achieves a 1.54% mean gap to the fluid lower bound, compared with 5.32% for a job-indexed adaptive large-neighborhood search and 6.18% for a hybrid genetic algorithm.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 本文采用理论推导的方法。给定一个极值集，作者构造性地证明从该极值集中删除一个悬垂点后，基于新极值集构造的Meissner多面体的最小表面积不超过原来的最小表面积。反之，向极值集中添加悬垂点后，新的最小表面积不小于原来的最小表面积。通过这两个不等关系，作者建立了悬垂点不影响最小表面积的结论。

Result: 证明了两个关键结论：(1) 删除悬垂点不会使基于生成集的Meissner多面体的最小表面积增大；(2) 添加悬垂点不会使最小表面积减小。由此得出，面积最小化的Meissner多面体的搜索可以限定在无悬垂点的生成集上。

Conclusion: 悬垂点在Meissner多面体表面积最小化问题中不是本质的（不具关键作用），可以安全地将其排除在搜索范围之外。这一结果简化了寻找面积最小Meissner多面体的算法与理论分析。

Abstract: Meissner polyhedra are constant-width bodies obtained from extremal finite sets of unit diameter. Such a generating set may contain dangling points, namely points having exactly two diametric neighbors. This article studies whether these points can play an essential role in surface-area minimization. Given an extremal set we show that the smallest surface area among the Meissner polyhedra based on it cannot increase by deleting a dangling point. Adding a dangling point cannot decrease the smallest achievable surface area. This reduces the search for area-minimizing Meissner polyhedra to generating sets without dangling points.

</details>

Abstract: This paper proposes a controller design method for shaping the controllability Gramian into a desired form to design the effect from exogenous inputs to the system state. In addition, by deriving a semidefinite programming formulation via a linear matrix inequality (LMI), computational efficiency is improved and additional LMI constraints can be incorporated. When the exogenous input is modeled as Gaussian white noise, the proposed framework is closely related to $H_2$ control, which can be interpreted as a special case of optimal transport. Numerical examples demonstrate anisotropic controllability design for a guidance robot and verify the ability to impose additional directional constraints through LMIs. The numerical examples also confirm that the proposed method approaches $H_2$ control as the desired Gramian tends to zero.

</details>

Method: 作者提出非线性残差线性化增广拉格朗日方法（NR-LALM）：(1) 用正则化Gauss-Newton型步骤替代非线性原始子问题，该步骤可由一个对称正定线性系统求解；(2) 保留基于非线性约束残差的经典乘子更新，但因此产生二阶约束线性化误差，该误差在局部正则性条件下可被控制；(3) 乘子有界性和轨迹局部化被证明而非假设；(4) 对于随机目标使用带保护重启的投影随机路径积分微分估计器；(5) 可选的最小范数二阶校正可将约束线性化误差从二阶降至四阶而不改变复杂度阶次；(6) 所有理论结果在Lean 4证明助手系统中形式化验证。

Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian methods are effective for nonlinear equality-constrained optimization, but solving their nonlinear primal subproblems can be expensive. For smooth nonconvex problems with deterministic or stochastic objectives, we propose a nonlinear-residual linearized augmented Lagrangian method (NR-LALM) that replaces this subproblem by a regularized Gauss-Newton-type step while retaining the classical multiplier update based on the nonlinear constraint residual. The resulting step is computed from one symmetric positive-definite linear system, but the mismatch between the linearized primal model and the nonlinear-residual update produces a quadratic constraint-linearization error in the multiplier identity. We show that this error can be controlled under local regularity; multiplier boundedness and trajectory localization are derived rather than assumed. With fixed, accuracy-independent parameters, deterministic NR-LALM finds an $\varepsilon$-approximate Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) pair in $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iterations and first-order oracle evaluations. For stochastic objectives, a projected stochastic path-integrated differential estimator with safeguarded restarts requires, in expectation, $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ stochastic-gradient evaluations and $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ constraint and Jacobian evaluations. Compactness and a Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz condition further yield finite-length convergence of the deterministic primal-dual sequence. An optional minimum-norm second-order correction reduces the constraint-linearization error from second to fourth order without changing the complexity orders. All theoretical results are formalized in Lean 4. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted error orders and show favorable performance on high-dimensional deterministic and stochastic problems.

</details>

Result: 推导出了保证成本降低的充分条件，构造了能够降低平均期望成本的策略，解析地确定了平均期望成本降低的下界，并通过仿真研究了实际可实现的成本降低程度。

Conclusion: 带控制休假的队列控制模型能够在满足所给充分条件的情况下降低平均期望成本，相比永久控制机制提供了一种更灵活的节省成本的替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce a model with control vacations instead of standard queueing control systems with permanent control. The researched model is an M/M/1 queue with temporary periods of service rate control with two available service rates. After a control period of exponentially distributed length, a control vacation is initiated during which a fixed service rate $μ$ is used. The start of the next control period needs to be scheduled directly at a certain cost. We will use the Markov Decision Process from Kanavetas et al. arXiv:2605.31573 to find a sufficient condition that ensures that the average expected cost can be reduced w.r.t. the model that only uses the fixed service rate. Under this condition we will use properties of this related process to construct a cost reducing policy. The process with control vacations under specific policies induces a renewal reward process. We use a Tauberian theorem to relate the average expected cost of this renewal reward process to a vanishing discount method and analytically determine a lower bound of the average expected cost reduction. Finally, we study the actual attained average cost reduction for these policies through simulation.

</details>

Abstract: This article develops a self-contained Lie-theoretic route from linear controllability to nonlinear controllability on matrix Lie groups. The organizing question is whether local algebraic information can be propagated into statements about reachable sets. The linear case supplies the model: the matrix exponential, Cayley-Hamilton theorem, trajectory formula and Kalman family B,AB,...,An-1B reduce controllability to finite-dimensional linear algebra. In the nonlinear setting, Lie brackets replace matrix powers, but the associated Lie algebra may be infinite dimensional and the finite-dimensional Lie correspondence can fail. Particular emphasis is placed on rotation groups. Skew-symmetric matrices, their exponentials, commutators, one-parameter subgroups, and the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff formula provide a concrete laboratory for seeing how infinitesimal directions propagate on SO(n). A numerical SO(3) simulation illustrates the geometry of controlled rotations, while explicit SO(4) and SO(7) commutator sequences make the propagation of a localized control direction visible. The article then develops the attainable-subgroup argument for right-invariant systems, gives a detailed proof sequence behind the Yamabe step, and works through solvable, nilpotent and ideal examples for upper-triangular matrix algebras. The finite-dimensional mechanism is finally contrasted with Sussmann's local controllability theory for general nonlinear systems.

</details>

Notably, this dual formulation bears a structural resemblance to classical barrier function techniques for certifying system safety, with the key distinction that it yields a probabilistic certificate. Abstract: We study continuous-time dynamic portfolio optimization under a Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) constraint on the investor's terminal loss. For a general class of convex trading objectives, we exploit the auxiliary-threshold representation of CVaR to establish the existence of an optimal strategy and strong duality without requiring market completeness. These results motivate a dual-based nested bisection--golden-search algorithm over the threshold and Lagrangian multiplier, where the inner iterations reduce to standard unconstrained stochastic control problems. We prove that the resulting strategies converge to the optimal control as the number of iterations tends to infinity. Numerical experiments recover the Merton policy when the risk constraint is nonbinding. When the constraint is binding, the optimal strategy becomes state dependent: the investor reduces risky exposure following adverse outcomes but preserves, and near maturity may increase, exposure following favorable outcomes. Thus, a terminal CVaR constraint produces an asymmetric reallocation across states rather than uniform de-risking. Nontraded endowment risk amplifies the conservative adjustment, whereas price impact lowers desired positions and adjustment speeds.

Abstract: A central methodological question in data-driven control is whether to adopt a direct or indirect approach. Direct methods infer a controller or certificate directly from data, while indirect methods first identify a system model and then apply model-based control techniques. Recent developments of the direct method have led to finite-sample guarantees for the data-driven stability analysis of switched linear systems under various settings. However, for the indirect method, such guarantees remain largely elusive. In this paper, we provide a novel framework for the stability analysis of switched linear systems from noisy state measurements, using the indirect approach and quadratic Lyapunov analysis. Our framework comes with finite-sample guarantees on the convergence rate of the system. For that, we combine generalization bounds from machine learning and system identification with sensitivity analysis from quadratic Lyapunov analysis. To enable comparison, we also extend existing direct data-driven methods to handle measurement noise beyond the bounded noise case currently available in the literature. Finally, we compare the two approaches through numerical experiments, revealing that under moderate-to-high noise levels the indirect approach yields tighter probabilistic guarantees as well as greater robustness to noise and outliers than the direct approach

Abstract: Directional subdifferentials of the value function provide a quantitative measure of optimal value response to perturbations. To address the lack of compactness of bounded sets in infinite-dimensional spaces, we introduce a new directional condition, under which we derive upper estimates for directional limiting and singular subdifferentials of the value function. These results provide a refined analytical foundation for sensitivity analysis in infinite-dimensional hierarchical systems.

We prove that a complete state equipped with this certificate admits a strict multiset replacement in a single dependent well-founded order; hence the iteration terminates and the exhausted state reconstructs a regular strict transform having normal crossings with the ordered boundary. We further formulate an object-level realization of the certificate through rigid generation, cross-generation no-reset, complete wild-capacity control, a centre-or-typed-exit alternative, structured cofibres, displayed-parent allocation, and literal terminal truth. Abstract: We propose a fully discrete method for linear fourth-order parabolic equations with Dirichlet boundary conditions, combining an implicit LBRFD multistep scheme in time with a mixed interior penalty discontinuous Galerkin (IPDG) method in space. Stability and a priori error estimates are established via a $G$-energy technique and the discrete Grönwall lemma. The theoretical analysis yields a total $L^2$ error estimate of order $h^{k-1}+τ^p$, where $p=d$ if $n-d$ is even and $p=d+1$ if $n-d$ is odd. The reduced spatial convergence rate is attributed to boundary contributions on $\partialΩ$. Despite this theoretical prediction, numerical experiments confirm the stability and demonstrate optimal convergence of order $h^{k+1}+τ^p$.

The HT format avoids this issue by decomposing the solution according to a binary tree consisting of bases for each dimension and core tensors which connect the bases. At each stage of the Runge-Kutta method, the bases computed at the previous stages are augmented to predict the upcoming basis and construct projection subspaces. By projecting onto these enriched subspaces, the bases and cores can be updated in a sequential manner going up the tree from leaf-to-root. Unlike the 3D Tucker method which has a single core tensor, the HT method also updates the intermediate core tensors. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the method observes high-order accuracy, and test how well the integrator captures the solution rank for various sets of time-dependent diffusion coefficients.

Abstract: We present a high-order radial basis function-generated finite difference (RBF-FD) method for partial differential equations on moving manifolds $\mathcal M(t)\subset\mathbb R^3$ of co-dimension one. Abstract: For given $m \times n$ data matrices $X, Y$, we investigate the Normal Procrustes Problem---the least squares optimization problem that aims to minimize $\|AX-Y\|_F^2$, where $A$ is constrained to be a normal $m \times m$ matrix. As far as the author of this article is aware, no other method that attempts to solve the Normal Procrustes Problem exists in the literature; we thus propose what is, to our knowledge, the first such method. We, furthermore, adapt our approach to address the Real Normal Procrustes Problem, where $A$ must be real. In our treatment of these problems, we first reduce our complex and real objective functions to be purely optimizable over the Riemannian manifolds of the unitary and real orthogonal matrices, respectively. This reduction enables us to apply techniques in Riemannian manifold optimization to approximate solutions to both. The Closest Normal Matrix and Real Closest Normal Matrix Problems are both special cases of their respective Procrustes Problems and have been previously studied in the literature. Our approach thus recovers a novel Riemannian optimization method for approximating solutions to both these problems. We further numerically test the performance of our method across all such problems (including against previously developed algorithms on the Closest Normal Matrix Problems) and obtain competitive residuals and favorable scaling in wall-clock time.

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for learning deterministic and stochastic forced Hamiltonian systems with neural networks. By interpreting the multiple Stratonovich integrals appearing in the Stratonovich-Taylor expansion as parameters, the same framework can be applied to stochastic forced Hamiltonian systems whenever information about the underlying Wiener process is available. Abstract: In this paper, we propose two hierarchical algorithms for charge deposition and electric-field interpolation that apply to both the sparse-grid combination technique (SGCT-PIC) and hierarchical sparse-grid (HSG-PIC) particle-in-cell methods. The arithmetic complexity of the charge deposition and field interpolation steps is reduced from $Ø(p^d n^{d-1}N)$ to $Ø(p^d(N+M))$, where $M=2^{dn}$ denotes the number of full-grid mesh nodes and is typically no larger than the particle population in the considered regime, $M\lesssim N$. Abstract: We extend Thorpe successive approximation expansion to the forced, tide locked internal wave generation problem of Lamb and Dunphy, where a barotropic tide over a ridge radiates a discrete spectrum of Taylor Goldstein eigenmodes in a steady shear current. The corrections peak at modes 2 to 3, grow quadratically with ridge height and current strength, and amount to a nonlinear slow-down of every mode, explaining from within the theory why the linear discrete spectrum model agreed so well with fully nonlinear simulations. Abstract: We present a sharp and conservative geometrical VOF--finite-volume method for multicomponent liquid--gas mass transfer across deformable interfaces. This avoids both volumetric regularization of interfacial source terms and explicit coupling based on previous-time-step interfacial data. A consistent geometrical advection scheme is used for volume, momentum, energy, and species transport, and a sequential coupling strategy is developed to determine the partial interfacial mass fluxes, close the temperature equation, and update the thermodynamic-equilibrium state. The method is validated through single- and multicomponent bubble dissolution, single-component droplet evaporation, non-ideal ethanol--isooctane droplet evaporation, and sessile water--glycerol droplet evaporation. The results demonstrate second-order accuracy, accurate interfacial flux prediction, good mass and energy conservation, and the ability to capture complex multicomponent effects such as gas replacement, azeotropic volatility reversal, and composition-driven Marangoni flow.

Abstract: The influence of microscale flow structures (smaller than the pore size) on turbulent heat transfer in porous media has not been yet investigated. The goal of this study is to determine the influence of the micro-vortices on convection heat transfer in turbulent porous media flow. Turbulent flow in a homogeneous porous medium was investigated using Large Eddy Simulation (LES) at a Reynolds number of 300. We observed that the convection heat transfer characteristics are dependent on whether the micro-vortices are attached or detached from the surface of the obstacle. There is a spectral correlation between the Nusselt number and the pressure instabilities due to vortex shedding. A secondary flow instability occurs due to high pressure regions forming periodically near the converging pathway between obstacles. This causes local adverse pressure gradient, affecting the flow velocity and convection heat transfer. This study has been performed for obstacles with shapes of square and circular cylinders at porosities of 0.50 and 0.87. Understanding the dominant modes that affect convection heat transfer can aid in finding an optimum geometry for the porous medium.

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics and stability of three immiscible viscous liquid layers coating the interior of a vertical cylindrical tube, a configuration relevant to stratified core--annular transport processes. A long-wave asymptotic analysis yields a coupled system of nonlinear evolution equations governing the motion of the three interfaces. Linear stability analysis predicts a persistent long-wave instability, the capillary (Rayleigh--Plateau) instability of the air--core interface, together with secondary finite-wavenumber instability bands that emerge from interfacial coupling in certain parameter regimes. These stability characteristics depend sensitively on the layer thicknesses, viscosity ratios, and surface tension parameters, and include mode-switching associated with competing maxima in the dispersion relation. Nonlinear simulations reveal three distinct dynamical outcomes: saturation to finite-amplitude travelling waves, air-core closure through plug formation, and rupture of the intermediate liquid layer while the air core remains open. The intermediate-layer rupture mechanism is unique to the three-layer configuration which has no analogue in one- or two-interface cylindrical film flows. Numerical continuation is used to compute branches of travelling-wave solutions and their associated limit points. Comparison with time-dependent simulations shows that travelling-wave branches successfully predict the transition from saturated waves to plug formation, but do not capture the distinct rupture mechanism associated with collapse of the intermediate layer.

</details>

Method: 构建TorchDCM：一个开源Python包，将选择数据和模型规格编译为统一的PyTorch原生似然引擎。涵盖多项、嵌套、混合、有序、潜变量和面板似然；支持不规则选择集、约束参数、协方差估计、支付意愿分析、弹性及可扩展似然组件；可在CPU或CUDA设备上运行。

Conclusion: TorchDCM为计量经济学估计和可微分的选择模型开发建立了可扩展且可复现的基础，弥合了传统计量经济工作流与可扩展微分计算之间的鸿沟，并以开源形式公开。

Abstract: Estimating large and simulation-intensive discrete choice models (DCMs) requires repeated evaluation of utilities, probabilities, derivatives, and simulated likelihoods over many observations, alternatives, and draws. Existing DCM software provides mature econometric workflows, while recent GPU-oriented tools accelerate selected models, leaving a gap between econometric coverage and scalable differentiable computation. We introduce TorchDCM, an open Python package for discrete choice modeling that compiles choice data and model specifications into a unified PyTorch-native likelihood engine for estimation, inference, prediction, and structured reporting on CPU or CUDA devices. The package covers the principal econometric functionality available across Biogeme and Apollo, including multinomial, nested, mixed, ordered, latent-variable, and panel likelihoods. It also supports ragged choice sets, constrained parameters, covariance estimation, willingness-to-pay analysis, elasticities, and extensible likelihood components. We evaluate TorchDCM against seven other estimation packages in aligned synthetic and real-data full-estimation experiments. TorchDCM completes all 45 synthetic cases, runs fastest in every comparable synthetic case, and satisfies the prespecified final-log-likelihood tolerance in every comparison with at least two comparable solutions. More precisely, it reduces median runtime by 89.1%-99.7% relative to Biogeme and Apollo across model-data settings. CUDA provides an additional 12.0-71.0x speedup over single-core TorchDCM. These results establish a scalable and reproducible foundation for econometric estimation and differentiable choice-model development. The open-source package and executed examples are available at https://github.com/mbc96325/torchdcm.

Abstract: We study inference-time control for text generation in discrete diffusion language models, where the goal is to steer sampling toward sequence-level rewards without retraining. Prior work in this domain has focused on particle-based methods such as best-of-$n$ sampling and bootstrap sequential Monte Carlo, which may suffer from overoptimism and weight degeneracy, respectively. We address these limitations using \emph{nested} sequential Monte Carlo methods. We formulate nested SMC (NSMC) and fully-adapted nested SMC (FA-NSMC) for Feynman--Kac steering, identifying and correcting errors in prior formulations that lead to biased final estimates. We evaluate these methods on toxicity and fluency steering tasks, showing that NSMC and FA-NSMC consistently outperform best-of-$n$ and bootstrap SMC.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出两阶段偏移学习框架：第一阶段将所有组的数据合并，估计一个总体均值函数（共享结构）；第二阶段分别估计每个组的偏移量（组特异偏差）；最终组级估计器通过共享均值与偏移量的加法组合得到。该框架可适用于广泛的非参数估计器，并在深度ReLU网络上实例化。

Abstract: This paper develops a general transfer learning framework for nonparametric regression with data consisting of multiple groups. Under the assumption that groups share a common structure along with group-specific deviations in additive form, the proposed method employs a two-stage offset learning procedure: the first stage pools data from all groups to estimate an overall mean function, and the second stage estimates offsets for each group, yielding final group-level estimators through additive combination. Upper bounds on the $\mathcal L_2$ error are established for the proposed framework, covering a broad class of nonparametric estimators under mild complexity and noise conditions. When instantiated with deep ReLU networks, explicit convergence rates are derived under hierarchical composition models, demonstrating the ability to overcome the curse of dimensionality. Conditions that enable positive transfer with faster rates are considered, including learning with simpler functions and data augmentation through pooling samples across groups. Various simulations and real-data experiments further validate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>

The resulting complex eigenfrequencies and eigenmodes are validated against exact analytical solutions and finite-element simulations, showing excellent quantitative agreement. The framework provides a systematic and physically transparent approach for analyzing perturbed open acoustic resonators and establishes a basis for resonant-state methods in acoustic metamaterials and non-Hermitian acoustics.

Abstract: We develop a resonant-state expansion formulation for acoustic scattering by individual resonators. The scattered pressure and particle-velocity fields are expanded over the resonant states of the system, with excitation amplitudes determined by overlap integrals between the incident field and the resonant states over the resonator volume. Using the acoustic energy flux, we derive expressions for the extinction, scattering, and absorption cross-sections and show that the extinction spectrum can be resolved into contributions from individual resonant states. The formulation is first validated for a homogeneous two-dimensional cylinder, where it reproduces the analytical Mie-theory solution. We then consider a sectorally perturbed cylinder with coupled azimuthal modes and demonstrate agreement with finite-element simulations. Finally, we combine the eigenvalue and scattering formulations for a material-programmed hard-wall annular metaatom and reproduce its scattering spectra and near fields. The developed framework provides a physically transparent modal approach to acoustic scattering by open resonators with reduced symmetry and spatially structured material parameters.

Abstract: We study a long-range perturbation of the perimeter functional by a nonlocal repulsive term defined through the Yukawa kernel, minimized under a volume constraint. Because the kernel decays exponentially, the competition between surface tension and repulsion is governed by two independent quantities, the screening rate and the volume. Specifically we prove that (i) above an explicit critical screening rate, minimizers exist at every volume, whereas earlier work required the volume to be large; (ii) below an explicit volume threshold minimizers exist for every screening rate; (iii) at small volume, and uniformly in the screening rate, the ball is the unique minimizer up to translation; and (iv) there exists a sharp volume threshold, depending on the screening rate, for the ball to be a stable volume-constrained critical point. Our proofs overcome new technical changes due to the lack of homogeneity in the Yukawa kernel.

Abstract: Let $D\subset\mathbb{R}^n$, $n\ge2$, be a bounded convex domain, and let $u_D$ be the torsion function for the restricted half-Laplacian. Abstract: In this article, we have studied a particular criterion to establish if a particular class of nonlinear PDEs $\mathcal{O}_t u=\mathcal{O}_x u $ in unidimensional space-time $(x,t)$ admits elementary solutions respect to the spatial coordinate $x$, through the nested derivative method. Where a nested derivative is a generalization of the usual partial derivative of a order higher than one and, in terms of differentials operators, is a generalization of the Laguerre operator. This method integrated with Liouville's theorem and Galois theory e method by integrate by parts establish if solution of nested derivative equations admits elementary solutions respect with $x$. These solutions could help to study better evolutions of possible physical systems in space-time.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有多孔洞结构的均匀旋转涡斑是平面欧拉方程涡动力学中的经典挑战。此前构造的多连通涡斑要么存在反号涡量，要么包含第二非零涡量层级，无法实现"真正的零涡量孔洞"。本文旨在突破这一限制，为任意指定数量的零涡量孔洞提供严格的存在性构造，并确保角速度处于与刚性相容的区间内。

Abstract: For every prescribed integer $N\ge2$, we construct uniformly rotating unit-vorticity vortex patches $ω=\mathbf{1}_D$ for the planar Euler equation such that $D$ is connected and $\mathbb{R}^2\backslash D$ has exactly $N$ bounded connected components. These components are genuine zero-vorticity holes, rather than opposite-sign vortex patches or regions carrying a second nonzero vorticity level. The angular velocities lie in the rigidity-compatible interval $(0,1/2)$, and the domains converge in measure to the Rankine disk as the holes collapse.
  The construction starts from a fixed co-rotating polygonal configuration of $N$ unit-vorticity vortex patches and removes a shrinking spatial copy of that configuration from the Rankine disk. An exact complement identity solves all inner-boundary equations before the outer circle is perturbed. Abstract: We construct and classify all global solutions to the singular equation $$-Δu=f(X)\cdot u^{-γ}$$ supported in a general Lipschitz epigraphical cone, where $f(X)$ is a locally Dini continuous function with $0<λ\leq f(X)\leqΛ$. The existence and non-existence of a global solution is solely determined by the exponent $γ$ of the equation and the ``frequency" of the cone.

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the dynamic stability of certain steady-state solutions to the periodic boundary value problem for compressible isentropic Navier-Stokes system under the van der Waals equation of state in one space dimension. Then, by virtue of rigorous piecewise a priori estimates, we demonstrate that the periodic boundary value problem for van der Waals fluids possesses a global solution existing for all time, and this solution converges uniformly to the admissible steady state as time tends to infinity. This result firmly establishes the nonlinear stability of the admissible phase-transition solutions under general small initial disturbances.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解聚焦临界NLS解的长时间渐近行为，特别是解的散射性质与孤子分解之间的关系，为后续利用集中紧性方法研究此类方程提供新工具。

Method: 证明包含两个关键要素：一是定理（锥散射）中局域化的低于基态的散射结果；二是定理（测度收敛）中对残余部分（减去散射部分后）渐近行为的刻画。此外还建立了升级机制：若解在时间序列上沿时空锥低于基态（或小），则在大时间下在收缩的时空锥内也一致低于基态（或小）。

Result: 证明了聚焦$H^{1/2}$临界NLS方程（d≥5）的解在有限多个任意小的时空锥之外发生散射，并给出了该结果与孤子分解之间的关系。作为副产品，建立了从时间序列上低于基态到一致低于基态的升级机制。

Conclusion: 该工作为临界NLS方程的散射理论提供了新的时空锥方法框架，所建立的升级机制有望在未来的集中紧性研究中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: In this article we prove that the solution to the focusing $H^{1/2}$-critical nonlinear Schrödinger equation in dimension $d\geq 5$ scatters outside finitely many arbitrarily small spacetime cones. We will discuss the relationship between Theorem~\ref{thm:finite-bad-directions} and the resolution of solitons. There are two key ingredients in the proof: the localized below-ground-state scattering in Theorem~\ref{thm:cone-scattering}, and the characterization in Theorem~\ref{thm:measure-convergence} of the asymptotic behaviour of the residual part (i.e., after subtracting the scattering part).
  As a byproduct, we also establish an upgrading machinery: if the solution is below the ground state (resp. small) along a time sequence in a spacetime cone, then it is also below ground state (resp. small) uniformly for large time in a shrinked spacetime cone. We expect this to be useful in future works using concentration compactness and our spacetime cone approach.

</details>


### [89] [Backstepping-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Wide-Range Saint-Venant Canal Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20089)
*Chenchen Wang,Jie Qi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 提出一种后推制导的软演员-评论家（SAC）框架，将基于模型的后推控制知识通过DeepONet嵌入强化学习的actor和critic网络中，并采用迁移学习策略保留有用知识，在Sambre河模型上相比纯后推控制在更大初始偏差下提升了调节性能和学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后推控制虽能为非线性Saint-Venant系统提供局部稳定性保证，但当系统偏离名义平衡点较远时，其调节性能可能显著退化，需要一种能融合模型控制知识与强化学习方法以提升鲁棒性的方案。

Method: 1) 用DeepONet学习名义后推控制律，并将其作为先验信息嵌入actor和critic网络的特征表示；2) 将所学先验与SAC策略结合生成最终控制输入；3) 采用迁移学习策略在适应非线性动力学过程中保留后推控制的有用知识。

Result: 在Sambre河模型上的仿真表明，所提方法相比纯后推控制方法提升了学习效率，并在更大的初始偏差范围内保持了有效的调节性能。

Conclusion: 通过将模型驱动的后推控制知识嵌入深度强化学习框架，可在保持局部稳定性的同时扩展系统有效调节的初始偏差范围，兼顾了模型知识的可靠性与强化学习的自适应性。

Abstract: Backstepping control provides local stability guarantees for nonlinear Saint-Venant systems, but its regulation performance may degrade when the system operates far from the nominal equilibrium. This letter proposes a backstepping-guided soft actor-critic (SAC) controller framework that incorporates model-based control knowledge into reinforcement learning (RL). The nominal backstepping control law is first learned by deep operator network (DeepONet) and embedded into the actor and critic networks as prior informed feature representations. The learned prior is further combined with the SAC policy to generate the final control input, while a transfer-learning strategy preserves the useful backstepping knowledge during adaptation to the nonlinear dynamics. Simulation results on the Sambre River model demonstrate that the proposed method improves learning efficiency and maintains effective regulation over larger initial deviations than backstepping control.

</details>


### [90] [An AI-discovered smooth random fast dynamo on $\mathbb{T}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20105)
*Keefer Rowan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个时间依赖、无散度的随机速度场（在有限时间块上iid刷新并满足确定性C^∞光滑界），证明了该速度场在三维环面上表现出快速发电机行为：对任意足够小的固定电阻率，磁场的几乎必然指数增长率至少为1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索快速发电机现象的理论机制，即流体运动如何在有限电阻率下放大磁场。传统的发电机问题涉及复杂的无穷维动力学，论文的动机是找到一种规避这种复杂性的代数结构，从而简化分析。

Method: 在Fourier空间中利用感应方程解算子的特定代数结构，选取三个特殊Fourier模式并传播其对数大小的期望增长，将问题化归为简单递归，从而避开无穷维动力学。此外还得到带随机前因子（满足关于κ一致逆矩界）的时间一致下界。

Result: 对每个固定且足够小的电阻率，磁场的几乎必然指数增长速率至少为1/2（例外零测集可以依赖电阻率）；同时获得时间一致的下界估计与关于电阻率的统一逆矩界。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个快速发电机的构造性证明，通过Fourier空间的代数结构简化了分析。值得注意的是，核心证明思想由ChatGPT 5.6 Sol Ultra自主生成，手稿由作者撰写并验证——这展示了AI辅助数学研究的可能性。

Abstract: We construct a random, time-dependent divergence-free velocity field on $\mathbb{T}^3$---refreshing iid on finite time blocks and obeying deterministic $C^\infty_{t,x}$ bounds---that exhibits fast dynamo behavior. That is, for every fixed, sufficiently small resistivity, the almost sure exponential growth rate of the magnetic field solving the associated linear resistive induction equation is at least $1/2$; the exceptional null set may depend on the resistivity. We in fact get a time-uniform lower bound---with a random prefactor obeying a uniform-in-$κ$ inverse moment bound. The argument relies on a particular algebraic structure in Fourier space of the induction equation solution operator that allows us to propagate expected growth of the logarithmic size of three specially chosen Fourier modes. This allows us to reduce to a simple recursion, avoiding the complicated infinite-dimensional dynamics typical to the dynamo problem. The central proof idea was generated autonomously by ChatGPT 5.6 Sol Ultra; the manuscript was written (and verified) by the author.

</details>


### [91] [A note on interior curvature estimates for strictly convex solutions to the equation of prescribed curvature quotient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20188)
*Bin Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类椭圆Weingarten曲率商方程严格凸解的先验内蕴曲率界，证明不依赖积分估计或紧致性论证，而是通过方程算子的凹性不等式和辅助函数的新选择，使用初等极大值原理完成。作为推论，得到了低维特殊Lagrangian曲率方程严格凸解的内蕴曲率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立椭圆Weingarten曲率商方程严格凸解的曲率界是理解其解的正则性与几何性质的关键。已有方法依赖复杂的积分估计或紧致性论证，作者希望提供更简洁、初等的证明途径。

Method: 利用方程算子满足的凹性不等式，并引入一种新颖的辅助函数构造，从而采用初等的极大值原理（maximum principle）论证来获得先验曲率界，绕过了积分估计和紧致性等高级技术手段。

Result: 成功证明了严格凸解的先验内蕴曲率界，并作为推论获得了低维特殊Lagrangian曲率方程相应解的曲率估计。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种更简洁、初等的证明框架，可推广到特殊Lagrangian曲率方程，为相关椭圆方程曲率估计问题提供了新的技术路线。

Abstract: In this note, we prove a priori interior curvature bounds for strictly convex solutions to elliptic Weingarten curvature quotient equations. The proof does not employ the advanced methods involving integral estimates or compactness arguments. Instead, it relies on a concavity inequality for the equation operator and a novel choice of the auxiliary function to carry out an elementary maximum-principle argument. Interior curvature estimates for strictly convex solutions to the special Lagrangian curvature equation in low dimensions also follow as a consequence.

</details>


### [92] [On the closed solution of a problem coupling fluid infiltration with a hydration reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20189)
*Diego Guevara,Sabrina Roscani,Piotr Rybka,Vaughan Voller*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个一维模型，用于描述水渗透与水合反应耦合的过程，该模型将饱和与干燥区域之间的尖锐界面视为随时间演化的移动自由边界问题，并给出了闭合解析解。该解表明，在早期时间，渗透前沿以时间平方根的规律推进；在后期，根据水合反应的消耗速率，前沿前进可呈现平方根、线性或指数三种不同的形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解地球近地表环境中耦合的传输与化学过程（如水的渗透和水合反应），需要建立一个可解析求解的理论模型，以量化自由边界在不同时间尺度下的演化行为，并验证以往数值方法的正确性。

Method: 构建一个一维渗透-水合反应耦合模型，将饱和区与干燥区之间的界面视为一个随时间演化的移动自由边界问题，并推导出该问题的闭合解析解。

Result: 解析解表明：在早期时间，渗透前沿以时间平方根（t^{1/2}）的形式推进；在后期，根据水合反应对水的消耗程度，前沿推进可表现为平方根、线性或指数三种不同的时间依赖形式，该结果与文献中已有的数值处理结果一致。

Conclusion: 该闭合解析解为量化耦合传输与反应系统中的关键行为提供了一个有效的分析工具，可用于验证数值方法并深入理解渗透前沿的动态演化规律。

Abstract: We present a one-dimensional model for water infiltration coupled with a hydration reaction, relevant to coupled transport and chemical processes in the Earth's subsurface. In this model the sharp interface separating the saturated and dry regions evolves over time, leading to a moving free boundary problem. Consistent with recently presented numerical treatments in the literature, this solution indicates an interesting dynamic for the free boundary. At early time, for a given domain porosity, the infiltration front advances with a specific square-root-in-time behavior. At later time, depending on the consumption of the hydration reaction, the front advance can exhibit square-root, linear, or exponential forms. The presented closed solution provides an analytical tool that can be used to quantify important behavior in coupled transport and reaction systems.

</details>


### [93] [Boundary layers and vanishing diffusivity in run-and-tumble models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20249)
*Dallas Albritton,Laurel Ohm,Timur Yastrzhembskiy*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文首次对受限活性物质系统中的非线性边界层进行了严格的数学分析，具体研究了半直线上一族具有非线性平流和翻转的1D run-and-tumble模型，证明了在扩散系数κ→0时带量化收敛率的极限定理，边界质量作为新变量满足非线性ODE并通过动态边界条件与PDE耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限活性物质系统的一个显著特征是运动粒子倾向于在固体边界附近聚集。在无通量边界条件的各类线性模型中，这种聚集通过小扩散系数κ下尖锐边界层的形成来实现。然而，此前缺乏对非线性边界层的严格数学论证，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 本文考虑半直线R_+上一族具有非线性平流和翻转的1D run-and-tumble模型，通过匹配渐近展开和边界层分析，严格证明了扩散系数κ→0时的极限定理，并给出量化收敛速率。极限系统中边界质量作为新变量进入，满足一个非线性ODE，并通过动态边界条件与PDE耦合。

Result: 研究证明了vanishing diffusivity极限（κ→0）成立且带有量化收敛速率。关键发现是：κ=0时边界上的非线性性不能仅通过对κ≪1的边界层分析以外的途径获得。数值实验表明这些模型表现出丰富的行为，包括边界层中的相变和迟滞现象。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了受限活性物质中非线性边界层的首个严格证明，揭示了边界非线性性与边界层内在结构之间的深层联系，并展示了系统中存在的相变和迟滞等丰富物理现象。

Abstract: A notable feature of confined active matter systems is the tendency for motile particles to accumulate near solid boundaries. In various linear models with no-flux boundary conditions, this accumulation is realized through the development of sharp boundary layers at small particle diffusivity $κ$. In this paper, we present the first rigorous investigation of nonlinear boundary layers in the context of confined active matter. Specifically, we consider a family of 1D run-and-tumble models with nonlinear advection and tumbling on the half-line $\mathbb{R}_+$. We rigorously prove the vanishing diffusivity limit with quantitative convergence rates. In the limiting system, the boundary mass enters as a new variable which solves a nonlinear ODE, coupled to the PDE through a dynamic boundary condition. Interestingly, the nonlinearity on the boundary at $κ= 0$ cannot be obtained without reference to the boundary layer analysis at $κ\ll 1$. Numerically, these models exhibit rich behavior, including phase transition and hysteresis in the boundary layer.

</details>


### [94] [Optimal regularity of stable harmonic maps to spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20272)
*Xuanyu Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了到k维球面的稳定驻调和映射的奇异集余维数下界：当3≤k≤6时至少为k+1，当k≥7时至少为7。该结果在临界维度上是最优的，因为存在能量极小化的0齐次映射达到此临界值。此外，还建立了当n<k时从n维球面到k维球面的非常数调和映射的非平凡指标界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和映射的奇异集结构是几何分析中的核心问题。此前关于稳定驻调和映射奇异集维数的估计主要针对一般目标流形，而针对球面这一特殊且重要的目标空间，可以获得更精确的余维数下界估计，并探索其最优性。

Method: 通过利用目标空间为球面的特殊几何结构（如等变对称性与周期间断类方法），结合稳定驻调和映射的变分性质和余维数分析方法，推导奇异集的余维数下界；同时构造临界维数下的能量极小化0齐次调和映射以证明结果的尖锐性；最后利用谱与指标理论建立调和映射的非平凡指标下界。

Result: (1) 当3≤k≤6时，稳定驻调和映射到k维球面的奇异集余维数至少为k+1；当k≥7时至少为7。(2) 该估计在临界维数下是最优的，存在达到此临界值的能量极小化0齐次映射。(3) 当n<k时，从n维球面到k维球面的非常数调和映射具有非平凡的指标下界。

Conclusion: 该结果改进了到球面的稳定驻调和映射奇异集的维数估计，并在临界维数下证明其最优性；同时为调和映射的指标理论提供了新的下界，深化了对球面间调和映射结构的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that the codimension of the singular set of a stable stationary harmonic map to a round $k$-sphere is at least $k+1$ when $k$ is between 3 and 6, and is at least 7 when $k$ is at least 7. The result is sharp in the sense that there exist energy minimizing 0-homogeneous maps in the aforementioned critical dimensions. We also establish non-trivial index bounds for the non-constant harmonic maps from $n$-spheres to $k$-spheres, provided that $n$ is less than $k$.

</details>

### [95] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于监督机器学习的直升机起飞重量估计模型，使用空客全球在役机队的大规模数据集进行训练，并面向航空安全关键功能部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直升机起飞重量是关键的飞行安全参数，传统的重量估计方法可能不够准确或无法满足实时性要求。航空领域正在推进机器学习应用的适航认证，需要一套符合监管要求的开发与验证流程。

Method: 采用监督机器学习方法，具体使用长短期记忆（LSTM）循环神经网络构建重量估计模型；提出一套机器学习需求（ML Requirements）和机器学习模型描述（ML Model Description）；实施了一个符合EASA概念文件和Eurocae ED-324标准的机器学习保障过程，并在老旧航电计算机上验证了系统实现。

Result: 该LSTM重量估计器成功在老旧航电计算机硬件上实现并部署，满足所提出的机器学习需求，验证了该实现适用于机上关键功能（如机载告警）的应用。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了机器学习模型在航空安全关键环境中落地的可行性，通过系统化的学习保障流程（对照EASA与Eurocae标准）确保模型可认证、可部署，为未来航空ML应用提供了参考路径。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [96] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的三角模糊数距离度量——Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_TR)，该度量将线性重缩放（LRE）直接集成到距离计算中，解决了异构属性（不同尺度或单位）下需要预先归一化的问题。论文正式证明了d_TR满足度量公理（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并展示了其有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性等优良性质，适用于合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策辅助等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的决策常涉及模糊信息，三角模糊数（TFNs）是常用的表示方式。现有的大多数距离度量假设所有数值处于同一尺度，在应用于具有不同尺度或单位的异构属性时需要预先进行归一化处理，这增加了计算复杂度且可能引入偏差。因此，需要一种能够直接在距离计算中完成归一化的距离度量。

Method: 本文提出d_TR度量，其核心创新是将线性重缩放（LRE）技术直接嵌入距离计算过程中，使得比较模糊数时的归一化自动完成。论文从数学上严格证明了d_TR满足度量空间的所有性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并通过证明其有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性来验证其理论完备性。此外，方法支持权重向量以实现维度优先级排序。

Result: d_TR被证明是一个正规度量，具备非负性、同一性、对称性和三角不等式等完备性质。同时，该度量是有界的，且具有尺度不变性和原点不变性，能够有效处理带权重的异构模糊数据距离计算，无需额外的预先归一化步骤。

Conclusion: d_TR作为一种集成了归一化功能的三角模糊数距离度量，为解决异构模糊数据的距离计算提供了理论支撑和实用工具。其良好的数学性质和权重支持使其在合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策辅助等应用中具有广阔前景。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [97] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大语言模型在动态心电图（ECG）分析中的不足，提出了Holtercare-23K大规模多模态动态ECG数据集和Holtercare-Bench基准，通过信号-视频-文本三模态对齐，评估模型在时序定位、临床诊断和全局总结方面的能力，发现现有MLLMs在超长病理序列处理上存在显著差距，而微调代表性模型可带来大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在医学应用中主要处理静态图像或短时信号，而在关键的动态心电图领域，模型因缺乏高质量数据集和基准而难以进行复杂的时序推理和诊断报告生成。

Method: 构建了Holtercare-23K大规模多模态动态ECG数据集，包含从788个临床Holter记录中衍生的22,980个QA对，提出信号-视频-文本三模态对齐；基于该数据集构建Holtercare-Bench基准，评估模型在时序定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个任务上的表现，并对代表性MLLMs进行零样本评估和微调实验。

Result: 零样本评估显示主流MLLMs在处理超长病理序列时存在显著性能差距；对代表性模型进行微调后获得了实质性性能提升。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了当前MLLMs在电生理学领域的局限性，并为面向长期医学场景的MLLMs提供了基础性基准。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [98] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用数据集来增强LLM不确定性量化（UQ）的方法。通过构建简单分类器，将已有的零样本UQ分数与相似查询的正确性作为特征，预测LLM响应的正确性，从而在部署前利用数据集持续超越现有UQ方法，且计算开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM不确定性量化方法（如口头化置信度和多生成采样法）大多是零样本的，无需标注数据即可产生不确定性分数。然而，在实际部署前必须评估这些方法在目标数据集上的表现，现有方法未能充分利用这一评估过程中获得的数据信号。

Method: 构建简单的分类器来预测LLM响应的正确性，特征包括：(1) 现有的零样本不确定性量化分数；(2) 相似查询的正确性信息。通过利用数据集中的标注信息作为特征，对分类器进行训练，从而增强原有的UQ分数。

Result: 该方法在利用数据集后能够一致地超越现有的零样本UQ分数，同时产生极小的计算开销，是一种廉价且直接有效的增强方案。

Conclusion: 通过利用部署前已有的数据集，将UQ问题转化为基于特征的二分类问题，可以用极低计算成本显著提升LLM不确定性量化的性能，适用于实际部署场景。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子经典混合机器学习在液体活检肺癌检测中的应用，利用DNA片段组学和DNA甲基化特征，通过量子核方法与SVM及逻辑回归结合，系统评估了不同特征编码和纠缠策略对分类性能的影响。结果表明量子核模型在片段组学数据集上优于经典SVM基线，在甲基化数据集上保持竞争力，支持量子核方法作为cfDNA肺癌检测的有前景途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量CT肺癌筛查虽能降低死亡率，但受限于依从性和管理挑战。基于cfDNA的生物标志物提供了互补方案，然而肺癌异质性和高维非线性分子信号使早期检测仍然困难。本文旨在探索量子经典混合机器学习是否能更好地捕捉cfDNA分子信号中的非线性结构，从而提升肺癌检测性能。

Method: 使用DNA片段组学和DNA甲基化特征，经特征选择后构建20和40特征子集。将特征通过角度和稠密角度特征映射编码到量子希尔伯特空间，采用多种纠缠策略。利用精确态矢量模拟计算基于保真度的量子核，并集成到预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归中，与原始特征上训练的经典SVM对比。通过重复留出评估系统性地考察编码和纠缠设计对分类的影响。

Result: 量子核模型在两个数据集上表现具有竞争力。碎片组学数据中，多个20特征配置较经典SVM基线提升了AUC，表明能有效捕捉cfDNA片段化的非线性结构。甲基化数据中经典SVM获得了最高AUC，但部分量子模型仍然具有竞争力并在某些情况下提升了特异性。特征数从20增至40未持续改善性能，反而增加变异性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法是cfDNA肺癌检测的有前景方向，尤其在片段组学数据上能够超越经典基线。研究结果强调特征工程和量子编码设计的系统评估对于实际应用的重要性，但特征数量增加带来的收益有限。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [100] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出DeltaMomentum，将方向感知能力直接融入动量更新规则。核心观察是线性层梯度可分解为作为key的输入和作为value的输出侧误差，利用这一键值结构，DeltaMomentum用canonical delta rule更新动量缓冲器，使每个方向按其出现频率被遗忘。该方法是任何优化器的即插即用替代品，在多项基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器（如AdamW）使用指数移动平均（EMA）形成动量，以固定速率遗忘所有方向。但深度网络在训练中看到的输入高度各向异性——少数方向被频繁查询，多数方向很少出现。近期方法试图通过在外围包裹额外处理来应对这种各向异性，但未改动动量更新本身。作者希望直接将方向感知能力构建进动量更新规则中。

Method: DeltaMomentum的核心在于观察到线性层梯度可拆分为：输入（作为key）和输出侧误差（作为value）。利用这一键值结构，动量缓冲器通过canonical delta rule更新，使得每个方向以与其出现频率相关的速率被遗忘。该方法无需矩阵求逆即可应用输入侧曲率校正，可作为任何优化器动量缓冲器的即插即用替代品。

Result: 实验表明：在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，DeltaAdamW相比AdamW在67M规模下节省高达46.39±4.32%的步数达到相同验证损失，370M规模下节省22.12±0.80%；1B规模在Chinchilla最优预算下增益仍持续。Muon基线在该协议下表现低于DeltaAdamW。在SGD、ResNet-18、ViT-Tiny和CIFAR-10上增益同样成立。额外计算量仅为gated-MLP块线性成本的22.2%-25.0%，且无持久内存开销。

Conclusion: DeltaMomentum通过将方向感知（key-value）结构融入动量更新本身，有效解决了深度学习中梯度分布各向异性导致的固定遗忘率问题。它被证明是有效动量、无需矩阵求逆即可做输入侧曲率校正、在固定与漂移最优下都比EMA更快清除过时方向。训练时诊断确认了其预测机制（更好的梯度跟踪和更健康的输入方向），且系数可在μP下跨宽度迁移。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget. A Muon baseline tuned under the same protocol sits above DeltaAdamW at both language-model scales, and the gain holds for SGD, ResNet-18, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10. Training-time diagnostics confirm the predicted mechanism, better gradient tracking and healthier input directions.

</details>


### [101] [Green BOA: Determining the environmental break-even point for ML-based data compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19994)
*Caterina Doglioni,Akshat Gupta,Thomas Elliott,Hanzila Hussain,Sanjiban Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文总结了曼彻斯特大学两个暑期实习项目的成果，聚焦于基于机器学习的数据压缩算法在环境可持续性方面的盈亏平衡点。以ML无损压缩算法为例，比较了ML训练和推理所需基础设施的碳当量与减少磁盘存储需求所节省的碳当量，并讨论了它们的盈亏平衡点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
